\n\nb7:T45d,

Given:

\n\n

Height of the right circular cone (h) = 24 cm

\n\n

Radius of the base (r) = 10.5 cm

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n\n

Volume of a right circular cone = (1/3) × π × r2 × h

\n\n

Take π = 22/7

\n\n

Given:

\n\n

The volume of bigger sphere = 8 times that of the smaller sphere

\n\n

Concept Used:

\n\n

The volume of sphere = \\(\\frac{4}{3}\\pi r^3\\)

\n\n

The surface area of sphere = \\(4\\pi r^2\\)

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{V1}{V2}=\\frac{8}{1}\\)दिया है:

\n\n

बड़े गोले का आयतन = छोटे गोले का 8 गुना

\n\n

प्रयुक्त अवधारणा:

\n\n

गोले का आयतन =  \\(\\frac{4}{3}\\pi r^3\\)

\n\n

गोले का पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल = \\(4\\pi r^2\\)

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{V1}{V2}=\\frac{8}{1}\\)

\n\n

⇒ r13 / r23 = \\(\\frac{8}{1}\\)

\n\n

⇒ मानों की तुलना करने पर,

\n\n

⇒ r1 / r2 = \\(\\frac{2}{1}\\)

\n\n

पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल के सूत्र में मान रखने पर,

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{S1}{S2}=\\frac{4\\pi (2)^2}{4\\pi (1)^2}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{S1}{S2}=\\frac{4}{1}=4:1\\)

\n\n

अतः, बड़े गोले के पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल का दूसरे गोले के पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल से अनुपात 4 : 1 है।

Given:

\n\n

A cuboid whose length is 20 cm, width is 15 cm, and height is 8 cm.

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

Total surface area of the cuboid = 2(length × width + length × height + width × height)

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Total surface area of the cuboid = 2(20 × 15 + 20 × 8 + 15 × 8)

\n\n

⇒ 2 × 580 = 1160 cm2

\n\n

∴ The total surface area of a cuboid whose length is 20 cm, width is 15 cm, and height is 8 cm is 1160 cm2.

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

Height of cylinder (h) = 10 cm

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

\n\n

Total Surface Area (TSA) = 2πrh + 2πr2

दिया गया है:

\n\n

बेलन का आधार व्यास (d) = 60 सेमी

\n\n

बेलन की ऊँचाई (h) = 10 सेमी

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

पार्श्व पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल (LSA) = 2πrh

\n\n

कुल पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल (TSA) = 2πrh + 2πr2

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

त्रिज्या (r) = d/2 = 60/2 = 30 सेमी

\n\n

LSA = 2πrh = 2xπx30x10 = 600π सेमी2

\n\n

TSA = 2πrh + 2πr2 = 2xπx30x10 + 2xπx302 = 600π + 1800π = 2400π सेमी2&l\nt;/sup>

\n\n

अनुपात (LSA : TSA) = 600π : 2400π

\n\n

⇒ 1 : 4

\n\n

∴ सही उत्तर विकल्प (4) है।

Given: Three metal cubes whose edges are 6 cm, 8 cm and 10 cm.

\n\n

Concept Used: Total volume will be constant.

\n\n

Volume of cube = (Edge)3 

\n\n

Calculation: 

\n\n

V1 = (6)3 = 216 cm3

\n\n

V2  = (8)3 = 512 cm3

\n\n

V3 = (10)3 = 1000 cm3

\n\n

Now, new cube volume V = V1 + V2 + V3  

\n\n

(Edge)3  = 216 + 512 + 1000

\n\n

(Edge)3 = 1728

\n\n

Edge = 12 cm

\n\n

So, Edge of new cube is 12 cm.

Given:

\n\n

Radius of base of Cylinder = diameter/2 = 28/2 = 14 cm

\n\n

Height of cylinder = 70 cm

\n\n

Concept used:

\n\n

Total surface area of cylinder = 2πrh + 2πr²

\n\n

Calculations:

\n\n

According to question,

\n\n

Total surface area of the cylinder is

\n\n

⇒ 2πrh + 2πr2

\n\n

⇒ 2 × (\\(\\frac{22}{7}\\)) × (14) × (70) + 2 × (\\(\\frac{22}{7}\\)) × (14)2

\n\n

⇒ 6160 + 1232

\n\n

⇒ 7392

\n\n

\\(\\therefore\\) Total surface area of Cylinder is 7392 cm2.

\n\n

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

प्रश्न के अनुसार,

\n\n

बेलन का कुल पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल है, 

\n\n

⇒ 2πrh + 2πr2

\n\n

⇒ 2 × (\\(\\frac{22}{7}\\)) × (14) × (70) + 2 × (\\(\\frac{22}{7}\\)) × (14)2

\n\n

⇒ 6160 + 1232

\n\n

⇒ 7392

\n\n

∴ बेलन का कुल पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल 7392 सेमी2 है।

Given:

\n\n

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

Surface area of cube = 6a2 

\n\n

Volume of cube = a3 

\n\n

Calculations:

\n\n

According to the question,

\n\n

Surface Area = 6a2 

\n\n

⇒ 54 = 6a2 

\n\n

⇒ a2 = \\(\\frac{54}{6}\\)

\n\n

⇒ a2 = 9

\n\n

⇒ a = \\(\\sqrt9\\) = 3 cm

\n\n

Now volume of cube

\n\n

V = (3)3 

\n\n

⇒the  V = 27 cm3 

\n\n

∴ Volume of the cube is 27 cm3.

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

"correct_option": "3", "solution_html": {"en": "

Formula Used : 

\n\n

Surface area of cube = 6a2

\n\n

Calculation : 

\n\n

Decrease in side = 12% = 12/100 = 3/25

\n\n

Original side length = 25

\n\n

New side length = 22

\n\n

Original Surface area = 6 × 252 = 3750

\n\n

New Surface area = 6 × 222 = 2904

\n\n

Decrease in surface area = 846/3750 × 100 = 22.56%

\n\n

∴ The correct answer is 22.56%.

\n", "hn": "\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

घन का पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल = 6a2

\n\n

गणना :

\n\n

भुजा में कमी = 12% = 12/100 = 3/25

\n\n

वास्तविक भुजा की लंबाई = 25

\n\n

नई भुजा की लंबाई = 22

\n\n

वास्तविक पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल = 6 × 252 = 3750

\n\n

नया पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल = 6 × 222 = 2904

\n\n

पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल में कमी = 846/3750 × 100 = 22.56%

\n\n

∴ सही उत्तर 22.56% है।

Shortcut Trick\n

Total Surface Area of cylinder = 2 × π × r(r + h)

\n

⇒ TSA = 2 × (22/7) × 21 × 26

\n

⇒ TSA = 44 × 3 × 26 = 3432 cm2

\n

∴ The correct answer is 3432 cm2.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Radius (r) = 21 cm

\n

Height (h) = 5 cm

\n

Formula Used:

\n

Total Surface Area of Cylinder = 2 × π × r × (r + h)

\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Total Surface Area = 2 × (22/7) × 21 × (21 + 5)

\n

⇒ Total Surface Area = 2 × 22 × 3 × 26

\n

⇒ Total Surface Area = 44 × 78

\n

⇒ Total Surface Area = 3432 cm2

\n

∴ The correct answer is 3432 cm2.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Curved Surface Area of a Cylinder

\n

The curved surface area includes only the lateral surface: CSA = 2 × π × r × h.

\n

Volume of a Cylinder

\n

The capacity of a right circular cylinder: Volume = π × r2 × h.

\n

Total Surface Area of a Cone

\n

TSA of a right circular cone = π × r(l + r), where l is the slant height (√(r2 + h2)).

Given:

\n\n

Ratio of volumes of two cylinders (V1 : V2) = 6 : 1

\n\n

Ratio of their heights (H1 : H2) = 1 : 6

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

V = πr2h, where r is the radius and h is the height of the cylinder.

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

V1/V2 = (πr1<", "hn": "

दिया गया है:

\n\n

दो बेलनों के आयतन का अनुपात (V1 : V2) = 6 : 1

\n\n

उनकी ऊँचाई का अनुपात (H1 : H2) = 1 : 6

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

V = πr2h, जहाँ r बेलन की त्रिज्या है और h ऊँचाई है। 

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

V1/V2 = (πr12H1) / (πr22H2) ---- (1)

\n\n

जैसा कि हमें अनुपात V1 : V2 = 6 : 1 और H1 : H2 = 1 : 6 दिया गया है, हम इन मानों को समीकरण (1) में निम्न प्रकार से प्रतिस्थापि", "te": "

ఇచ్చినది:

\n\n

రెండు సిలిండర్ల ఘనపరిమాణాల నిష్పత్తి (V1 : V2) = 6 : 1

\n\n

వాటి ఎత్తుల నిష్పత్తి (H1 : H2) = 1 : 6

\n\n

ఉపయోగించిన సూత్రం:

\n\n

V = πr2 h, ఇక్కడ r అనేది వ్యాసార్థం మరియు h అనేది సిలిండర్ యొక్క ఎత్తు.

\n\n

గణన:

\n\n

V1/V2 = (πr12 H1) / (πr22 H2) ---- (1)

\n\n

మనకు V1 : V2 = 6 : 1 మరియు H1 : H2 = 1 : 6 నిష్పత్తులు ఇవ్వబడినందున, మనం ఈ విలువలను సమీకరణంగా మార్చవచ్చు (1):

\n\n

6/1 = (r12 × 1) / (r22 × 6)

\n\n

r1/r2 కోసం ఈ సమీకరణాన్ని పరిష్కరిస్తే, మనకు లభిస్తుంది:

\n\n

r1/r2 = √((6 × 6) / 1)

\n\n

r1/r2 = √(36)

\n\n

r1/r2 = 6

\n\n

కాబట్టి, రెండు సిలిండర్ల వ్యాసార్దాల నిష్పత్తి 6 : 1.

\n", "mr": "

दिलेल्याप्रमाणे:

\n\n

दोन वृत्तचितीच्या घनफळाचे गुणोत्तर (V1 : V2) = 6 : 1

\n\n

त्यांच्या उंचीचे गुणोत्तर  (H1 : H2) = 1 : 6

\n\n

वापरलेले सूत्र:

\n\n

V = πr2h, जेथे r ही त्रिज्या आहे आणि h ही वृत्तचितीची उंची आहे.

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

V1/V2 = (πr12H1) / (πr22H2) ---- (1)

\n\n

आपल्याला V1 : V2 = 6 : 1 आणि H1 : H2 = 1 : 6 असे गुणोत्तर दिलेले आहेत, आपण ही मूल्ये समीकरणात (1) बदलू शकतो:

\n\n

6/1 = (r12 × 1) / (r22 × 6)

\n\n

r1/r2 साठी हे समीकरण सोडवल्यास आपल्याला मिळते:

\n\n

r1/r2 = √((6 × 6) / 1)

\n\n

r1/r2 = √(36)

\n\n

r1/r2 = 6

\n\n

म्हणून, दोन वृत्तचितीच्या त्रिज्याचे गुणोत्तर 6 : 1 आहे.

প্রদত্ত:

\n\n

দুটি চোঙের আয়তনের অনুপাত (V1 : V2) = 6 : 1

\n\n

তাদের উচ্চতার অনুপাত (H1 : H2) = 1 : 6

\n\n

অনুসৃত সূত্র:

\n\n

V = πr2h, যেখানে r হল ব্যাসার্ধ এবং h হল চোঙের উচ্চতা।

\n\n

গণনা:

\n\n

V1/V2 = (πr12H1) / (πr22H2) ---- (1)

\n\n

যেমন আমাদের অনুপাত দেওয়া হয়েছে V1 : V2 = 6 : 1 এবং H1 : H2 = 1 : 6, আমরা এই মানগুলিকে সমীকরণ આપેલ:

\n\n

બે નળાકારના ક્ષેત્રફળોનો ગુણોત્તર (V1 : V2) = 6 : 1

\n\n

તેમની ઊંચાઈનો ગુણોત્તર (H1 : H2) = 1 : 6

\n\n

વપરાયેલ સૂત્ર: 

\n\n

V = πr2h, જ્યાં r એ ત્રિજ્યા છે અને h એ નળાકારની ઊંચાઈ છે.

\n\n

ગણતરી:

\n\n

V1/V2 = (πr12H1) / (πr22H2) ---- (1)

\n\n

જેમકે આપણને V1 : V2 = 6 : 1 અને H1 : H2 = 1 : 6 ગુણોત્તર આપવામાં આવ્યા છે, આપણે આ મૂલ્યોને સમીકરણ (1) માં બદલી શકીએ છીએ:

\n\n

6/1 = (r12 × 1) / (r22 × 6)

\n\n

r1/r2 માટે આ સમીકરણ ઉકેલવાથી, આપણને મળે છે:

\n\n

r1/r2 = √((6 × 6) / 1)

\n\n

r1/r2 = √(36)

\n\n

r1/r2 = 6

\n\n

તેથી, બે નળાકરોની ત્રિજ્યાનો ગુણોત્તર 6 : 1 છે.

ਦਿੱਤਾ ਗਿਆ:

\n\n

ਉਹਨਾਂ ਦੀਆਂ ਉਚਾਈਆਂ ਦਾ ਅਨੁਪਾਤ (H1 : H2) = 1 : 6

\n\n

ਵਰਤਿਆ ਗਿਆ ਸੂਤਰ:

\n\n

V = πr2h, ਜਿੱਥੇ r ਸਿਲੰਡਰ ਦਾ ਅਰਧ-ਵਿਆਸ ਹੈ ਅਤੇ h ਉਚਾਈ ਹੈ।

Given:

\n\n

Capacity of the cylinder tank = 8316 cm3

\n\n

Radius of the base (r) = 21 cm

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n\n

Volume of a cylinder = πr2h

\n\n

Where, π (pi) ≈ 22/7, r = radius, h = height (or depth)

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Volume = πr2h

\n\n

8316 = (22/7) × 212 × h

\n\n

⇒ 8316 = (22/7) × 441 × h

\n\n

⇒ 8316 = 22 × 63 × h

\n\n

⇒ 8316 = 1386h

\n\n

⇒ h = 8316 / 1386

\n\n

⇒ h = 6 cm

\n\n

The depth of the tank is 6 cm.

"options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "ਦੋ"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "ਚਾਰ"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "ਅੱਠ"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "ਇੱਕ"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "3", "solution_html": {"en": "\n Shortcut Trick\n\n

Volume of a sphere is proportional to the cube of its radius (V ∝ r3).

\n\n

Initial radius ratio = 1 : 2

\n\n

Volume ratio = 13 : 23

\n\n

Volume ratio = 1 : 8

\n\n

The volume becomes 8 times the previous volume.

\n\n

∴ The correct answer is eight.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n\n

Given:

\n\n

Initial radius = r1

\n\n

Final radius = r2 = 2 × r1

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n\n

Volume of Sphere (V) = (4/3) × π × r3

\n1 = (4/3)πr3 r → 2r 2r V2 = (4/3)π(2r)3 = 8V1 \n\n

Calculations:

\n\n

⇒ Let initial volume be V1 = (4/3) × π × r13

\n\n

⇒ Let final volume be V2 = (4/3) × π × r23

\n\n

⇒ Substitute r2 = 2r1 in the volume formula:

\n\n

⇒ V2 = (4/3) × π × (2r1)3

\n\n

⇒ V2 = (4/3) × π × 8r13

\n\n

⇒ V2 = 8 × [(4/3) × π × r13]

\n\n

⇒ V2 = 8 × V1

\n\n

∴ The correct answer is eight.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n\n

Surface Area of Sphere

\n\n

The total surface area of a sphere is given by A = 4 × π × r2. If radius is doubled, the surface area becomes 4 times.

\n\n

Volume of Hemisphere

\n\n

The volume of a hemisphere is exactly half of a sphere: V = (2/3) × π × r3.

\n\n

Hemisphere Surface Areas

\n\n

Curved Surface Area (CSA) = 2 × π × r2 | Total Surface Area (TSA) = 3 × π × r2.

\n", "hn": "\n शॉर्टकट ट्रिक\n\n

गोले का आयतन उसकी त्रिज्या के घन के समानुपाती होता है (V ∝ r3)।

\n\n

प्रारंभिक त्रिज्या अनुपात = 1 : 2

\n\n

आयतन अनुपात = 13 : 23

\n\n

आयतन अनुपात = 1 : 8

\n\n

आयतन पिछले आयतन का 8 गुना हो जाता है।

\n\n

∴ सही उत्तर आठ है।

\n\n

\n\n

दिया गया है:

\n\n

प्रारंभिक त्रिज्या = r1

\n\n

अंतिम त्रिज्या = r2 = 2 x r1

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

गोले का आयतन (V) = (4/3) x π x r3

\n1 = (4/3)πr3 r → 2r 2r V2 = (4/3)π(2r)3 = 8V1 \n\n

गणनाएँ:

\n\n

⇒ माना प्रारंभिक आयतन V1 = (4/3) x π x r13

\n\n

⇒ माना अंतिम आयतन V2 = (4/3) x π x r23

\n\n

⇒ आयतन सूत्र में r2 = 2r1 रखने पर:

\n\n

⇒ V2 = (4/3) x π x (2r1)3

\n\n

⇒ V2 = (4/3) x π x 8r13

\n\n

⇒ V2 = 8 x [(4/3) x π x r13]

\n\n

⇒ V2 = 8 x V1

\n\n

∴ सही उत्तर आठ है।

\n\n

अतिरिक्त जानकारी

\n\n

गोले का पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल

\n\n

गोले का कुल पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल A = 4 x π x r2 द्वारा दिया जाता है। यदि त्रिज्या दोगुनी कर दी जाए, तो पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल 4 गुना हो जाता है।

\n\n

अर्धगोले का आयतन

\n\n

एक अर्धगोले का आयतन एक गोले का ठीक आधा होता है: V = (2/3) x π x r3।

\n\n

अर्धगोले के पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल

\n\n

घुमावदार पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल (CSA) = 2 x π x r2 | कुल पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल (TSA) = 3 x π x r2।

Given:

\n\n

The heights of two cones are in the ratio 5:3.

\n\n

The radii of two cones are in the ratio 6:5.

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n\n

Volume of a cone = \\( \\frac{1}{3} \\pi r^2 h \\)

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Let the height of the first cone be 5h and the height of the second cone be 3h.

\n\n

Let the radius of the first cone be 6r and the radius of the second cone be 5r.

\n\n

Volume of the first cone = \\( \\frac{1}{3} \\pi (6r)^2 (5h) \\)

\n\n

Volume of the first cone = \\( \\frac{1}{3} \\pi (36r^2) (5h) \\)

\n\n

Volume of the first cone = \\( \\frac{1}{3} \\pi (180r^2h) \\)

दिलेले आहे:

\n\n

संकल्पना:

\n\n

गोलाचे घनफळ V = (4/3)πr³ या सूत्राद्वारे दिली जाते, जेथे r ही त्रिज्या असते.

\n\n

वापरलेले सूत्र:

\n\n

गोलाचे घनफळ: V = (4/3)πr³

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

प्रारंभिक त्रिज्या r समजू.

\n\n

प्रारंभिक घनफळ, Vप्रारंभिक = (4/3)πr³

\n\n

त्रिज्या दुप्पट केल्यावर, नवीन त्रिज्या = 2r.

\n\n

नवीन घनफळ, Vनवीन = (4/3)π(2r)³

\n\n

⇒ Vनवीन = (4/3)π(8r³)

\n\n

⇒ Vनवीन = 8 × (4/3)πr³

\n\n

⇒ Vनवीन = 8 × Vप्रारंभिक

\n\n

∴ त्रिज्या दुप्पट केल्यावर गोलाचे घनफळ आधीच्या घनफळाच्या 8 पट होते.

Given:

\n\n

\n\n

Concept:

\n\n

ਇੱਕ ਗੋਲੇ ਦਾ ਆਇਤਨ ਫਾਰਮੂਲੇ V = (4/3)πr³ ਦੁਆਰਾ ਦਿੱਤਾ ਜਾਂਦਾ ਹੈ, ਜਿੱਥੇ r ਅਰਧ ਵਿਆਸ ਹੈ।

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n\n

ਗੋਲੇ ਦਾ ਆਇਤਨ: V = (4/3)πr³

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

ਮੰਨ ਲਓ ਕਿ ਸ਼ੁਰੂਆਤੀ ਅਰਧ ਵਿਆਸ r ਹੈ।

\n\n

\n\n

ਜਦੋਂ ਅਰਧ ਵਿਆਸ ਦੁੱਗਣਾ ਹੋ ਜਾਂਦਾ ਹੈ, ਤਾਂ ਨਵਾਂ ਅਰਧ ਵਿਆਸ = 2r।

\n\n

ਨਵਾਂ ਆਇਤਨ, Vਨਵਾਂ = (4/3)π(2r)³

\n\n

⇒ Vਨਵਾਂ = (4/3)π(8r³)

\n\n

⇒ Vਨਵਾਂ = 8 x (4/3)πr³

\n\n

⇒ Vਨਵਾਂ = 8 x Vਸ਼ੁਰੂਆਤੀ

\n\n

\n\n

Ratio of volume before and after = 500 : 486

\n\n

Again multiply by 2 in = 1000 : 972

\n\n

Volume decreased by = 1000 – 972 = 28

\n\n

As we know, 28 = 2.8%

\n\n

Detailed Solution:

\n\n

Let the radius of the cylinder be r cm and height of the cylinder be h cm,

\n\n

As we know,

\n\n

Volume of the cylinder = πr2h

\n\n

New radius of the cylinder = r × (90/100) = 0.9r cm

\n\n

New height of the cylinder = h × (120/100) = 1.2h cm

\n\n

New volume of the cylinder = π × (0.9r)2 × 1.2h = 0.81r2 × 1.2h × π = 0.972rhπ

\n\n

Required percentage = [(πr2h - 0.972πrh)/πrh] × 100 = 2.8%

The radius of a right circular cylinder is five times its height.

\n\n

The height of the cylinder is 3.5 cm

\n\n

Concept used:

\n\n

Volume of a cylinder = πr2h

\n\n

here,

\n\n

r = radius and h = height

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

The radius of the cylinder = 3.5 × 5

\n\n

⇒ 17.5

\n\n

Now,

\n\n

Volume = π × 17.52 × 3.5

\n\n

⇒ 22/7 × 17.5 × 17.5 × 3.5

\n\n

⇒ 22 × 2.5 × 17.5 × 3.5

\n\n

⇒ 3368.75

\n\n

∴ The volume of the cylinder is 3368.75 cm3.

Given:

\n\n

Radius of the sphere (r) = 14 cm

\n\n

Painting cost per square centimetre = Rs. 5

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n\n

Surface area of a sphere = 4πr2

\n\n

Total cost = Surface area × Painting cost per square centimetre

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Surface area of the sphere = 4πr2

\n\n

⇒ Surface area = 4 × (22/7) × 142

\n\n

⇒ Surface area = 4 × (22/7) × 196

\n\n

⇒ Surface area = 4 × 22 × 28

\n\n

⇒ Surface area = 2464 cm2

\n\n

Total cost = Surface area × Painting cost per square centimetre

\n\n

⇒ Total cost = 2464 × 5

\n\n

⇒ Total cost = Rs. 12,320

\n\n

The cost of painting the ball is Rs. 12,320.

\n", "hn": "

दिया गया है:

\n\n

गोले की त्रिज्या (r) = 14 cm

\n\n

प्रति वर्ग सेंटीमीटर पेंटिंग की लागत = 5 रुपये

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

गोले का पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल = 4πr2

\n\n

कुल लागत = पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल x प्रति वर्ग सेंटीमीटर पेंटिंग की लागत

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

गोले का पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल = 4πr2

\n\n

⇒ पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल = 4 x (22/7) x 142

\n\n

⇒ पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल = 4 x (22/7) x 196

\n\n

⇒ पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल = 4 x 22 x 28

\n\n

⇒ पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल = 2464 cm2

\n\n

कुल लागत = पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल x प्रति वर्ग सेंटीमीटर पेंटिंग की लागत

\n\n

⇒ कुल लागत = 2464 x 5

\n\n

⇒ कुल लागत = 12,320 रुपये

\n\n

गेंद को पेंट करने की अभीष्ट लागत 12,320 रुपये है।

\nbe:T529,

Given:

\n\n

Radius (r) = 6 cm

\n\n

Slant height (l) = 10 cm

\n\n

We need the height (h) of the cone to find the volume. Use the Pythagorean theorem:

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

h = √(l2 - r2)

\n\n

Volume of the cone = (1/3) × π × r2 × h

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Given:

\n\n

Volume = \\(\\frac{256}{3}\\pi\\) cm3

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

Volume = (4/3)π r3

\n\n

Curve surface area = 4π r2

\n\n

Solution:

\n\n

Let the radius of sphere be r cm.

\n\n

⇒ (4/3)π r3 = \\(\\frac{256}{3}\\pi\\)

\n\n

⇒ r3 = 64

\n\n

⇒ r = 4 cm

\n\n

⇒ Total surface area = 4π × r2

\n\n

⇒ Total surface area = 4π × 16

\n\n

⇒ Total surface area = 64π cm2

\n\n

∴ Option 2 is the correct answer.

⇒ r = 4 cm

\n\n

⇒ कुल पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल = 4π × r2

\n\n

⇒ कुल पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल = 4π × 16

\n\n

⇒ कुल पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल = 64π cm2

\n\n

∴ विकल्प 2 सही उत्तर है।

\n\na7:T96e,\n
